patiḥ | tasyodagayanaṃ śuklo'haḥ pūrvāhnava | tadevānām |

daharahaḥ | athāpyatrodāharanti - yasyeme vartmanī havyakavye
vahato devā yasyā, pitaro'rdha tathaiva nimeṣā yasya mūlaṃ,
taṃ ṛtavo vai yajantamamṛtatvamiyāt ||
śuklaśva tāmisra pakṣau | yasmāddevānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ caivaṃ
vibhaktaḥ kālaḥ, tasmāt kṛstnasya kālasyānenaiva prakāra-
dvayena saṃgṛhītatvāt 'nānyabhāvaḥ' nānyathātvaṃ, tadūvyāmi-
śratvāt pūrvāhnāparāhṇādibhedena | 'aharaharubhayathāpi ijyante '
prakāradvayena pūrvāhvāparāṅkhādibhedena | 'ime vartmanī' devayāna-
pitṛyānākhye | etasmādarthavādavākyāt sāmānyataḥ śrāddha-
kartavyatāvidhiḥ kalpyate ||

ahardinam | tadetatsaṃvatsaṃrāvayavajātaṃ devānām | dakṣiṇāyanaṃ saṃvatsarāvayavajāta

chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ | [2,81 - 92]
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṣīṇe rājani śasyate |'
vāsarasya tṛtīyāṃśe nātisandhyāsamīpataḥ ||
yadā caturdaśīyāmaṃ turīyamanupūrayet |
amāvāsyā kṣīyamāṇā tadaiva śrāddhamiṣyate ||
yaduktaṃ yadahastveva darśanaṃ naiti candramāḥ |
tatkṣayāpekṣayā jñeyaṃ kṣīṇe rājani cetyapi ||
yathoktaṃ dṛśyamāne'pi tacaturdaśyapekṣayā |
amāvāsyāṃ pratīkṣeta tadante vāpi nirvapet ||
aṣṭame'śe caturdaśyāḥ kṣīṇo bhavati candramāḥ |
amāvāsyāṣṭamāṃśe ca punaḥ kila bhavedaṇuḥ ||
āgrahāyaṇyamāvāsyā tathā jyeṣṭhasya yā bhavet |
viśeṣamābhyāṃ bruvate candravāravido janāḥ ||
atrendurāgraprahare'vatiṣṭhate caturthabhāgonakalāvaśiṣṭaḥ |
tadanta eva kṣayameti kṛtsnamevaṃ jyotiścakravido vadanti ||
yasminnande dvādaśaikaśca yatrya-
stasmiṃstṛtīyā paridṛśyā na jāyate ||
evaṃ cāraṃ candramaso viditvā
kṣīṇe tasminnaparā ca dadyāt ||
saṃmiśrā yā caturdaśyā amāvāsyā bhavet kacit |
svarṣitāṃ tāṃ viduḥ kecidupedhvamiti cāpare ||
vardhamānāmamāvāsyāṃ lakṣayedapare'hani |
yāmāṃstrīnadhikān vāpi pitṛyajñastato bhavet ||
pakṣādāveva kurvīta sadā pakṣādikaṃ carum |
pūrvāhna eva kurvanti viddhe'pyanye manīṣiṇaḥ ||
(1) piṇḍapitṛyajñāṅgabhūtānāṃ piṇḍānāmanu paścādāhiyate kriyate iti
'piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṣīṇe' 'rājani' indau kartavyam || (smṛti candrikā, p. 307, )

nī na bhavati tadāyaṃ pakṣo boddhavyaḥ | itarathā tu prathama
eva pakṣa: | 'amāvāsyā'ṣṭamāṃśa' iti yavā'māvāsyā-
yaṣṭamo'śo'parāyāpanayogyo bhavatyaparadine tavāpya-
śurūpasya candramamo vidyamānatvāt śrayābhāvāna
āddhaṃ kartavyam | ata eva hetoryatrāmāvāsyāyā na vṛddhi
kṣayau, kiṃ tu viṣṭambheno apavinagoraparāhnasaṃbandhāmāvāsyā-
saṃbhavatayā pūrvadina evaṃ śrāddhaṃ karmavyam | śrīṇa rājani
śasyata' iti manuvacanāt | āgrahāyaṇamyaṃprāmāvāsyayoḥ

svadhāmantraṃ ca tattasmātebhyaḥ priyataraṃ sadā |

atha vai camasādibhyastvardhapātrebhya eva ca |

brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu śaktyā dadyāttu dakṣiṇām |

śvaḥ kariṣya iti jñātvā brāhmaṇāmantraṇakriyā ||

127
'gandhamantraḥ' - 'sarvaḥ sugandhaḥ' | 'puṣpamantraḥ-
'śriyā devyā' | 'dhūpamantro' 'bṛhasātirasān' |
tato vastrāṇi viprāya sarvāṇyābharaṇāni ca |
punaḥ pakkānnakaṃ caiva kalpayeca vasundhare ||
triṣu varṇeṣu kartavyaṃ pākaṃ bhojanameva ca |
śuśrūṣāmabhipannānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca varānane ||
dine trayodaśe prāpte pākena bhojayedvijān |
evaṃ vidhiḥ prayoktavyaḥ śūdrāṇāṃ mantravarjitaḥ ||
amantrasya ca śūdrasya vipro mantreṇa gṛhyate |
bhujyamānasya vipreṇa pretabhāgasya sarvaśaḥ ||
jñātivargeṣu mitreṣu sambandhisvajaneṣu ca |
bhāgastasya pradātavyo yenārtho yasya vidyate ||
śucirbhūtvā tu vidhivat kṛtvā śāntyudakāni ca |
praṇamya śirasā devi nivāpasthānamāgatam ||
stotavyāni ca mantrāṇi samabhaktyā vyavasthitam |
mantraḥ-
namo namo medini lokadhātri
urvi mahi śailagiridhāriṇi dharaṇe namaḥ |
dharaṇi kāśyapi jagatpratiṣṭhe
vasudhe namo'stu vaiṣṇava bhūtadhātri ||
namo'stu te sarvarasapratiṣṭhe-
nivāpanāvīti namo namo'stu te |
dadyāttilodakaṃ tasya nāmagotramudāharan ||
[[page 248-to-248]]
śrāddhakāṇḍe ekoddiṣṭam
saṃvāhya caraṇau tasya namaskṛtya dvijottamam |
saṃgṛhya pāṇinā pāṇi mantreṇotthāpayedvijam ||
dadyācchayyāsanaṃ devi tathaivāñjanakāṅkatam |
añjanaṃ kaṅkataṃ gṛhya śayyāmākramya sa dvijaḥ ||
muhūrta tatra viśramya pāvanasthānamāgatam ||
gavāṃ lāṅgūlamuddhṛtya brāhmaṇo hastamādadhat |
pātra audumbare tatra kṛtvā caiva tilodakam ||
upāharanti mantreṇa sāmavedādvijātayaḥ |
mantraiḥ pūrvaṃ tadā toyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
gṛhya caudumbaraṃ pātraṃ vipreṇa niyatātmanā |
mantreṇābhyukṣayetsarvaṃ svagṛhe yattu tiṣṭhati ||
devāṃścāgnimukhān sarvāstarpayitvā vibhāgaśaḥ |
bhūtānāṃ ca baliṃ dadyādbrāhmaṇebhyaśca bhojanam ||
pretabhāgena dattena pūjayitvā dvijātayaḥ |
mṛtasya nāmatoddiśya hyakṣayaṃ copakalpayet ||
svasti vācya ca viprāya dadyātpretavisarjanam |
prāśayet pañcagavyaṃ ca madhuparkeṇa saṃyutam ||
śrīrāmāyaṇe'
samatīte daśāhe tu kṛtaśauco vidhānataḥ |
